MobySanityContainer-module-scss-module__VJkCGq__hero" data-hero-nav="away"><div class="stage"><h1 class="MobyHero-module-scss-module__rdpyvG__visually-hidden MobyHero-module-scss-module__rdpyvG__heading" id="mh-hero-title">Claude Sonnet 5.5</h1><svg id="mh-ov" aria-hidden="true"><defs><filter id="mh-title-glow" x="-8%" y="-40%" width="116%" height="180%" color-interpolation-filters="sRGB"><feGaussianBlur in="SourceAlpha" stdDeviation="14" result="b"></feGaussianBlur><feFlood flood-color="#c9d4ff" flood-opacity=".16"></feFlood><feComposite in2="b" 2026</p><nav aria-label="Contents"><ol class="toc"><li><a href="#introduction"><span class="n">(1)</span><span class="dots"></span><span>Introduction</span></a></li><li><a href="#performance"><span type="button" class="skipbtn scrollcue">Scroll down</button></div></section><article class="MobySanityContainer-module-scss-module__VJkCGq__article"><div class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__body LaunchBody-module-scss-module__JsRY2q__launch-body" data-theme="ivory"><span id="introduction" class="LaunchBody-module-scss-module__JsRY2q__anchor"></span><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__article-summary">Introducing Claude Sonnet 5.5, the second model in the Claude 5.5 family. It’s a clear upgrade over Claude Sonnet 5, runs 30%+ faster, and costs up to 30% less for most work.</p><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text">Sonnet 5.5 is a faster, lower-cost complement to Claude Opus 5.5. Where Opus 5.5 is built for complex work requiring careful judgment, Sonnet 5.5 is strongest at well-scoped everyday tasks, fixing bugs, and creating polished documents, slides, and spreadsheets. It’s also got a sharp eye for design. Claude Haiku 5.5, built for high-volume and cost-sensitive applications, will join the Claude 5.5 family in the coming weeks.</p><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text">Sonnet 5.5 improves over Sonnet 5 on:</p><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text"><strong>Performance.</strong> Sonnet 5.5 scores 70.6% on Terminal-Bench 4.0, an agentic coding evaluation, compared to Sonnet 5’s 10.3%. It scores two points below Opus 5.5 on GDPval-AA, a test of real-world work across a variety of occupations. And it’s strong on long-horizon work and image understanding—it’s the first Sonnet model to beat <em>Pokémon Red</em> working only from screenshots.</p><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text"><strong>Collaboration.</strong> Like Opus 5.5, Sonnet 5.5 writes more clearly than our previous generation of models; early testers described it as a better partner for collaboration than Sonnet 5. Its speed also makes it well suited to fast iteration on less complex tasks.</p><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text"><strong>Cost.</strong> Sonnet 5.5 is priced the same as Sonnet 5 at $2 per million input tokens, $10 per million output tokens, and $0.20 per million tokens for cache reads, but it typically needs far fewer tokens to do the same work. In our testing, it costs up to 30% less per task than its predecessor.</p><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text"><strong>Speed.</strong> Sonnet 5.5 generates outputs 30%+ faster than Sonnet 5, making it our fastest Sonnet model to date.</p><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text"><strong>Alignment and safety.</strong> On our automated behavioral audit, Sonnet 5.5 improves on or matches Sonnet 5 on most measures of alignment. Because its cybersecurity capabilities are comparable to Opus 5’s, it’s the first Sonnet model to launch with cyber safeguards and fallbacks like those we’ve developed for our most capable models. Its biology safeguards are the same as Sonnet 5’s. Both safeguards target a narrow set of high-risk requests; routine software development and most life sciences work are unaffected.</p><h2 class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column headline-4 serif post-heading" id="performance">Performance</h2><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text">Sonnet 5.5 improves on Sonnet 5 across domains—in some cases dramatically. On several evaluations, Sonnet 5.5 at Max effort even performs comparably to Opus 5.5. However, benchmark scores capture only one facet of a model’s capabilities; in our own testing, and in that of external testers, Opus 5.5 remains clearly stronger at complex, open-ended work requiring sustained judgment.</p><div class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__media-column"><figure class="BenchmarkGrid-module-scss-module__zKqBAG__figure BenchmarkGrid-module-scss-module__zKqBAG__cloud"><div class="BenchmarkGrid-module-scss-module__zKqBAG__wrap"><div class="BenchmarkGrid-module-scss-module__zKqBAG__layer"><table class="BenchmarkGrid-module-scss-module__zKqBAG__table"><thead><tr><td class="BenchmarkGrid-module-scss-module__zKqBAG__corner"></td><th scope="col" class="BenchmarkGrid-module-scss-module__zKqBAG__subject BenchmarkGrid-module-scss-module__zKqBAG__pinned">Sonnet 5.5</th><th scope="col" class="">Sonnet 5</th><th scope="col" class="">Opus 5.5</th><th scope="col" class="">GPT-6 Sol</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr against its cost per task at every effort level. As effort goes up, models typically work for longer, leading to a higher cost per task but generally also a higher score. The closer a point is to the top left of the chart, the more capability it delivers per dollar.</p><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text">On several benchmarks, Sonnet 5.5 at Low or Medium effort beats Sonnet 5’s best score for about a tenth of the cost per task. It complements Opus 5.5 best when running at lower effort settings, where it costs less per task. At higher settings, it can perform comparably at a similar cost.</p><div class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__media-column"><section class="ViewSwitcher-module-scss-module__kN30kq__root"><div post-heading" id="coding">Coding</h2><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text">Sonnet 5.5’s jump in performance is particularly noticeable in coding. At High effort on FrontierCode, it scores 10 points higher than Sonnet 5 at the same setting, at about one fifteenth of the cost per task. On CursorBench, which tests models on tasks from real Cursor coding sessions, its best score is within about two points of Opus 5.5.</p><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text">Early testers appreciated how quickly Sonnet 5.5 can understand a codebase. They were also struck by its efficiency: in head-to-head runs, it batched tool calls together more than Sonnet 5, leading to fewer steps and lower costs.</p><div I’m impressed with Sonnet 5.5’s handling of long-running, complex tasks.”</p></blockquote></div><div class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__split"><div class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__cell"><span class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__label">Company</span><span class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__value">Creator</span></div><div class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__cell"><span class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__label">Author</span><span class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__value">Kevin Ngo, Creative Coder</span></div></div></article></div></div><div class="TestimonialTabs-module-scss-module__1oJwFW__veil TestimonialTabs-module-scss-module__1oJwFW__veil-left" aria-hidden="true"></div><div class="TestimonialTabs-module-scss-module__1oJwFW__veil TestimonialTabs-module-scss-module__1oJwFW__veil-right" aria-hidden="true"></div></div></section></div><h2 class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column headline-4 serif post-heading" id="knowledge-work">Knowledge work</h2><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text">Sonnet 5.5 shows gains in multiple areas of knowledge work. On GDPval-AA, which tests models on real-world tasks across 44 occupations and nine major industries, Sonnet 5.5 scores nearly level with Opus 5.5 and about 400 points above Sonnet 5. It’s close to Opus 5.5 in computer use and chart recognition, and clearly outperforms Sonnet 5 and GPT-6 Sol on long-horizon knowledge work.</p><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text">Early testers highlighted less quantifiable improvements. They found it to be a more natural conversational partner and remarked on its knack for design, noting that it adds polish to user interfaces and can follow slide templates to create decks that require minimal editing. In one internal test, we gave it a public company’s quarterly earnings materials and call transcripts, along with a slide template, and asked for a 10-slide operating review. Two experts judged its first draft to be ready to send as is.</p><div class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__media-column"><section class="TestimonialTabs-module-scss-module__1oJwFW__root"><div For everyday coding and higher-effort conversations, that means faster iteration and a smoother build loop.”</p></blockquote></div><div class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__split"><div class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__cell"><span class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__label">Company</span><span class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__value">Lovable</span></div><div class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__cell"><span class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__label">Author</span><span class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__value">Fabian Hedin, Co-founder and CTO</span></div></div></article></div></div><div class="TestimonialTabs-module-scss-module__1oJwFW__slide" data-slide="far-next"><div class="TestimonialTabs-module-scss-module__1oJwFW__panel" id="_R_1geqnpfibrb_-panel-5" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="_R_1geqnpfibrb_-tab-5" aria-hidden="true" inert=""><article class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__card TestimonialTabs-module-scss-module__1oJwFW__card"><div class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__body"><span class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__label">Quote</span><blockquote class="TestimonialCard-module-scss-module__16pwma__quote"><p class="body-2 serif">“With millions of Rovo-assisted actions powering our customers’ workflows each month, execution speed is critical. class="ModelComparison-module-scss-module__mnfOdW__toggle" role="group"><button type="button" class="body-3" aria-pressed="false">Sonnet 5</button><button type="button" class="body-3" aria-pressed="true">Sonnet 5.5</button></div></div></section></div><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text">Adjusting the <a href="https://academy.claude.com/tutorials/choosing-the-right-effort-level-in-claude-code" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">effort level</a> lets you balance cost and speed against overall quality. In Claude Code and our apps, the default effort is set to Medium, while the Claude Platform defaults to High. At lower settings, Claude answers faster and uses fewer tokens, which suits routine work. At higher settings, Claude reasons for longer and checks its work more thoroughly.</p><h2 class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column headline-4 serif post-heading" id="safety">Safety</h2><h3 class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column headline-5 serif post-section" id="alignment">Alignment</h3><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text">Sonnet 5.5 doesn’t advance the frontier of our models’ capabilities, so our alignment assessment focused on a targeted set of risks that apply to models of any capability level, including acting against users’ interests, misleading users, and cooperating with high-stakes misuse.</p><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text">On our automated behavioral audit, which tests Claude across roughly 1,850 scenarios, Sonnet 5.5 improves on or matches Sonnet 5 on most measures of alignment, resistance to misuse, and honesty. On our newer containment evaluations, Sonnet 5.5 comes close to Opus 5.5, the best model we tested, in how rarely it tries to escape its sandbox, and it’s the least likely of any of our models to probe the limits of its containers. Across the full audit, Opus 5.5 still performs slightly better overall, but we found no evidence that Sonnet 5.5 pursues goals that conflict with the user’s intention.</p><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text">As we described in our <a href="https://www.anthropic.com/research/alignment-assessment-cybersecurity-incidents" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">recent alignment assessment</a>, no set of evaluations reliably catches every failure, and Sonnet 5.5 may have tendencies we haven’t found, which is why we pair our own alignment work with the safeguards described below.</p><h3 class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column headline-5 serif post-section" id="safeguards">Safeguards</h3><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text"><strong>Cybersecurity.</strong> Sonnet 5.5’s cyber capabilities are a large improvement over Sonnet 5’s, so we’re deploying it with safeguards similar to those on Opus 5.5. Users can still find and fix bugs in their code as part of routine software development, but higher-risk cybersecurity tasks will visibly fall back to Sonnet 5. Soon, cyberdefenders will be able to apply to our expanded <a href="https://support.claude.com/en/articles/14604842-real-time-cyber-safeguards-on-claude-opus-and-sonnet" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Cyber Verification Program</a> for tiered access to more advanced capabilities on Sonnet 5.5, Opus 5.5, and Claude Mythos models.</p><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text"><strong>Biology.</strong> Sonnet 5.5 uses the same set of biology safeguards as Sonnet 5. These target harmful requests; most research, education, and clinical work is unaffected, though some microbiology and virology requests may be flagged in error. Organizations can apply to our <a href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/life-sciences-verification-program" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Life Sciences Verification Program</a> for access to safeguards designed for the full breadth of biology-related work.</p><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text"><strong>Distillation.</strong> Distillation attacks, in which attackers use thousands of fake accounts to extract a model’s capabilities at industrial scale, allow bad actors to create highly capable models without the safeguards we build into Claude. Because Sonnet 5.5 is far more capable than its predecessor, it’s the first Sonnet model to launch with safety classifiers that prevent reasoning extraction. Sonnet 5.5 also expands <a href="https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/build-with-claude/preserved-thinking" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">preserved thinking</a>, so Claude’s thinking cannot be decoupled from the account that created it. Most developers won’t notice a change. If you move conversations between accounts, including switching accounts mid-session in Claude Code, our <a href="https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/build-with-claude/preserved-thinking" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">docs article</a> explains the change.</p><h2 class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column headline-4 serif post-heading" id="availability">Getting started</h2><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text">As with Opus 5.5 and Sonnet 5, Claude Sonnet 5.5 is available with zero data retention.</p><p class="Body-module-scss-module__z40yvW__reading-column body-2 serif post-text">Claude Sonnet 5.5 is now available on all platforms, including Amazon Web Services, Google Cloud, and Microsoft Azure. Developers can get started on the Claude Platform with <code class="InlineCodeBlock-module-scss-module__nsPAba__code">claude-sonnet-5-5</code>. If you run Sonnet with thinking off, you’ll need to switch to the new <code class="InlineCodeBlock-module-scss-module__nsPAba__code">between_tools</code> setting, which keeps up-front thinking off, before moving to Sonnet 5.5. See our <a href="https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/models/sonnet-5-5/migration-guide#turn-thinking-off" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">migration guide</a> for details.</p></div><section id="footnotes" class="MobyFootnotes-module-scss-module__qyiXDa__footnotes"><h2 class="headline-5 serif MobyFootnotes-module-scss-module__qyiXDa__heading">Footnotes</h2><div class="MobyFootnotes-module-scss-module__qyiXDa__columns"><p class="MobyFootnotes-module-scss-module__qyiXDa__note"><sup class="MobyFootnotes-module-scss-module__qyiXDa__label">1</sup> <!-- -->Terminal-Bench 4.0 results are reported for Claude Opus 5.5 at Xhigh effort which represents the model’s highest score.</p><p class="MobyFootnotes-module-scss-module__qyiXDa__note"><sup class="MobyFootnotes-module-scss-module__qyiXDa__label">2</sup> <!-- -->Sonnet 5.5 scores lower at Max effort than at Xhigh. FrontierCode evaluates whether a code change could be merged without human edits. It penalizes out-of-scope changes, even if they are high-quality or helpful. At Max effort, Sonnet 5.5 more often ran Claude Code’s code-review skill, which splits the review across many subagents, and in two cases Cognition examined, this led to a timeout or to extra edits beyond the task’s scope, and therefore to a lower score.</p><p class="MobyFootnotes-module-scss-module__qyiXDa__note"><sup class="MobyFootnotes-module-scss-module__qyiXDa__label">3</sup> <!-- -->Artificial Analysis ran GDPval-AA and AA-Briefcase on a pre-release deployment of Sonnet 5.5 on the Claude Platform, which we found to have a bug that could degrade responses to requests that use structured outputs. We expect the effect on Sonnet 5.5’s scores, if any, to be small and to understate its performance. That bug has since been fixed.</p><p class="MobyFootnotes-module-scss-module__qyiXDa__note"><sup class="MobyFootnotes-module-scss-module__qyiXDa__label">4</sup> <!-- -->OpenAI recently fixed a bug that degraded image understanding in GPT-6 Sol. Official AA-Briefcase v1.1 and GDPval-AA v2.1 scores from Artificial Analysis, and Chartography scores from Surge AI, may not have been updated yet to reflect the latest version of the model. Artificial Analysis does not expect major impacts to AA-Briefcase v1.1 and GDPval-AA v2.1. Internal testing of Chartography suggests its score was not impacted.</p></div></section></article></div></main><footer id="footer" class="SiteFooter-module-scss-module__JdOqwq__root"
